COMPANY PROFILE
企業簡介

Midea Real Estate Holding Limited (the “Company” and together with its                美 的 置 業 控 股 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）及 其 附 屬
subsidiaries, the “Group” or “Midea Real Estate”) (Stock Code: 3990.HK) is            公 司（「本 集 團」）或（「美 的 置 業」）   （股 份 代 號：
a listed company of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock               3990.HK），是香港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）
Exchange”), whose shares are included in the constituents of indexes                  上市企業，被納入恒生港股通等成分指數。本集
such as Hang Seng Stock Connect Hong Kong Index. Founded in 2004, the                 團創立於 2004 年，以「智慧健康生活服務商」的發
Group, guided by the development positioning as a “Smart Healthy Life                 展定位為指引，構建「開發服務 + 物管服務 + 資產
Service Provider”, has built comprehensive development ecology of “project            運 營 + 房 地 產 科 技」的 複 合 型 發 展 生 態，持 續 向
management services + property management services + asset operation +                高質量發展穩健邁進。
real estate technology”, and continued to move steadily towards high-quality
development.

Midea Real Estate Services, upholding service-oriented original aspiration, ranks     「美置服務」秉持服務本質初心，憑藉製造業匠心
among the top 18 property service companies in China with its craftsmanship            位列中國物業服務力 TOP18 企業，構建了以住宅
in manufacturing. It established three core business sectors of residential,           為基礎、產業園及醫療康養為核心的三大業態並
industrial parks and medical care and wellness, supplemented by diversified            疊加新能源、團餐等多元化賽道，截止報告期末，
tracks such as new energy and group meals. As at the end of the Reporting              合約項目逾 400 個，合約面積約 9,000 萬平方米。
Period, there were over 400 projects under management with an area under              「美置建設」是一家具備多業態綜合管理能力及全
management of approximately 90 million square metres. Midea Real Estate                周期定制服務能力的專業開發服務商，擁有豐富
Construction is a professional project management service provider capable             的項目管理經驗，堅持為客戶提供綠色節能、健
of managing integrated multi-business sectors and customising full-cycle               康多能的高品質開發服務。「美置商業」與「美置
services with extensive project management experience and is committed                 產發」聚焦強化輕資產經營能力，通過運營創新
to providing customers with high-quality project management services                   開拓第三方業務，做好客戶經營，實現有質量的
that are green and energy-saving as well as healthy and multi-functional.              增長。「睿住發展」作為承載公司轉型升級戰略的
Midea Real Estate Commercial and Midea Real Estate Property Development                房地產科技產業，圍繞設計科技、建築工業化、
focused on strengthening its light-asset operation capabilities, developed             人工智能家居三大領域，打造建築全生命周期數
third-party business through innovated operation, and maintained good                  字化、綠色化、工業化、智慧化能力，致力於成
relationship with customers to achieve quality growth. REMAC ASPACE, as the            為智慧健康空間科技的領軍企業。
real estate technology industry in which the Company adopts its strategies
for transformation and upgrade, focused on three areas covering design

healthy space technology.

        動工暨項目簽約儀式圓滿成功
        Amid high expectations, the successful groundbreaking and project
        signing ceremony for the Nanhai Ruichuang, an industrial park
        developed by Midea Real Estate Property Development, marked a
        significant milestone. The Nanhai Ruichuang Center aims to drive industrial upgrades and smartly shape a brighter future!
        盛載各方期許，美置產發旗下產業園南海睿創中心動工暨項目簽約儀式圓滿功成。南海睿創中心助躍產
        業升級，智領美好未來！

APR
4月
        Midea Real Estate Services Partnered with
        Heyou International Hospital, Setting a New
        Benchmark for Medical Services in the Bay Area
        美置服務：攜手和祐國際醫院，打造灣區醫
        療服務新標桿
        Midea Real Estate Services provides comprehensive property
        management for Heyou International Hospital, managing a total
        service area of approximately 600,000 square meters. Upholding
        the philosophy of “Original Aspiration and Craftsmanship, Quality
        Leads to Beauty,” Midea Real Estate Services is committed to
        elevating the hospital’s high-quality development.
        美置服務為和祐國際醫院提供全線物業綜合管理服務，總服務面積約 60 萬平方米。美置服務秉承初心匠心 •
        質領美好的服務理念，助力和祐國際醫院高質量發展。

5月
      REMAC ASPACE’s MiC Modular Integrated Construction
      System Earns IPA Certification from Hong Kong Buildings
      Department
      睿住發展：MiC 模塊化集成建築系統獲香港屋宇署 IPA 認證
      The MiC modular industrial construction system under REMAC ASPACE has received
      IPA certification from the Hong Kong Buildings Department, enabling solutions for
      buildings up to 40 stories tall.
      睿住發展旗下裝配式產業 MiC 模塊化集成建築系統獲得香港屋宇署 IPA 認證
      可為 40 層（以下）建築提供解決方案。




JUN
6月
      REMAC ASPACE Attended the 12th China
      International Urban Construction Exposition,
      Exploring New Trends in Green and Smart
      Manufacturing
      睿住發展：亮相第 12 屆中國國際城市建設博
      覽會，綠色裝配智造，探索未來空間新趨勢
      At the 12th China International Urban Construction Exposition in
      Beijing, REMAC ASPACE unveiled its “REMAC Smart Building 137
      System” ( 睿住智造好房子 137 體系 ), and its achievements, which
      are built on digitalisation, industrialisation, and green technologies.
      第 12 屆中國國際城市建設博覽會在京舉行，睿住發展展示了基於數字化、工業化、綠色化技術打造的「睿
      住智造好房子 137 體系」和示範成果。

8月
        Midea Real Estate Services Awarded 2024 Top 22 Property
        Service Companies by Comprehensive Strength in China
        美置服務：榮獲 2024 中國物業綜合實力 TOP22
        In the 2024 rankings of comprehensive strength of property service companies in
        China, Midea Real Estate Services stands out with exceptional service capabilities,
        earning recognition as the “2024 TOP 22 Property Service Companies by
        Comprehensive Strength in China”. Meanwhile, the Company also received important
        awards such as the ‘Leading Residential Property Service Enterprises in China’ and
        ‘Leading Property Service Enterprises for Industrial Parks in China”.
        2024 年中國物業服務企業綜合實力榜單發佈，美置服務憑藉卓越服務力榮鷹
        2024 中國物業服務企業綜合實力 Top22。同步獲得中國住宅物業服務領先企
        業、中國產業園區物業服務領先企業等重要獎項。




NOV
11月
        Midea Real Estate Services — Midea Real
        Estate Meikang Explored Healthcare and
        Wellness Logistics with a Successful Project
        Bid in Shenzhen
        美置服務：美置美康探索醫養後勤服務，中
        標深圳項目
        Midea Real Estate Meikang, a professional healthcare and wellness
        services company under Midea Real Estate Services, has successfully
        won the bid for the property services project for the new and old
        campuses of Shenzhen Zhongshan Obstetrics and Gynecology Hospital. Midea Real Estate Meikang is equipped with a
        professional service team to provide attentive and quality services with full-dimensional upgrades. The pre-construction
        team intervenes in the construction site in advance and conduct acceptance inspections to ensure the construction of a
        safe, comfortable and efficient medical environment.
        美置服務旗下專業醫療康養服務公司美置美康成功中標深圳中山婦產醫院新老院區物業服務項目。美置美
        康配備專業服務團隊提供悉心優質服務，配置全維度升級。工程前介團隊提前介入施工現場，開展承接查驗，
        為建設一個安全、舒適、高效的醫療環境保駕護航。

                                                             美的置業控股有限公司     2024 年度報告         9
CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告

Dear shareholders:                                                                尊敬的股東：

I am pleased to present to you the annual report of the Group for the year        本 人 欣 然 向 閣 下 提 呈 本 集 團 截 至 2024 年 12 月
ended 31 December 2024 (the “Reporting Period”).                                  31 日止年度（「報告期」）的年度報告。

I.    Industry Overview                                                           一、行業概述
      2024 has quietly passed, and within the calm flow of time, change is            2024 年，又 在 悄 然 中 過 去，平 靜 的 時 間 流
      always brewing. Under the overarching theme of “seeking progress                水 中 總 是 在 孕 育 著 變 化。中 國 房 地 產 市 場
      amidst stability”, China’s real estate market has entered a new phase           也在「穩中求變」的總基調下，進入深度調
      characterised by parallel processes of deep adjustment and structural           整 與 結 構 性 轉 型 並 行 的 新 階 段。政 策 托 底
      transformation. “Policy support” and “market differentiation” have              與 市 場 分 化 成 為 關 鍵 詞，行 業 逐 步 從 規 模
      become key themes, as the industry gradually shifts from scale expansion        擴張轉向高質量發展。
      to high-quality development.

      Faced with cyclical bottom fluctuations in the real estate sector and           面對房地產行業的週期底部震蕩及市場逐
      gradual market polarisation, embracing change has become both an                步 分 化，求 變，既 是 企 業 生 存 的 本 能，也
      instinct for corporate survival and a reconstruction of dual capabilities       是對抗風險與穩增長雙重能力的重新構建。
      to combat risks while maintaining stable growth. Yet this is no easy            但這絕非易事，魄力與決心，責任與擔當，

      priorities is expected to achieve higher profit margins and deliver more             持續的回報。

Interest Rate Risk                                                                 利率風險
The Group’s interest rate risk arises from interest-bearing bank deposits and      本集團的利率風險來自計息銀行存款及其他借款。
other borrowings. Bank deposits, bank and other borrowings at variable rates       按浮動利率計息之銀行存款、銀行及其他借款令
expose the Group to cash flow interest rate risk. Bank and other borrowings at     本集團面臨現金流量利率風險。按固定利率計息
fixed rates expose the Group to fair value interest rate risk.                     之銀行及其他借款令本集團面臨公平值利率風險。

management will continue to pay attention to the foreign exchange risk and          風險，並採取審慎措施，以減低匯兌風險。
adopt prudent measures to minimize the exchange risk.

available to it. The Group has maintained strict credit assessment procedures          集團一直維持嚴格的信貸評估程序以核實借款人

objectives. The Board also believes that this structure would not impair the     會與本公司管理層之間的權力及權限平衡，此乃
balance of power and authority between the Board and the management of           因為董事會由九名經驗豐富且具有良好誠信的高
the Company because the Board comprises nine experienced and high-calibre        素質人才組成，其中三名為獨立非執行董事，彼

The Company Secretary plays an important role in supporting the Board by           公司秘書擔任協助董事會的重要角色，以確保董

and procedures are duly followed. The Company Secretary reports to the Board       公司秘書透過主席向董事會匯報企業管治事務，
through the Chairman on corporate governance matters while all Directors           而所有董事均可向公司秘書取得建議及服務。
have access to the advice and services of the Company Secretary.

management and internal control systems of the Group and reviewing the              內部監控系統及檢討該等系統的有效性。本公司
effectiveness of these systems. The Company adopts risk management and              採納風險管理及內部監控系統以加強與管理層的
internal control systems to enhance the accountability and communication            問責及與其交流已識別風險、評估已識別風險的
of identified risks with management, measure the impact of the identified           影響及積極促進實施減低風險的措施。該等系統
risks and facilitate implementation of mitigation measures proactively. These       旨 在 提 供 合 理（而 非 絕 對）保 證 並 無 重 大 失 實 陳
systems are designed to provide reasonable, but not absolute, assurance             述或損失，以及管理（而非消除）未能達成本集團
against material misstatement or loss, and to manage, but not eliminate, risks      目標的風險。

Risk Management and Internal Control Systems                                        風險管理及內部監控系統

structure with specified limits of authority and responsibility. Each of the        權限及責任的清晰架構。各業務單位作為風險預
business departments acts as the primary unit of risk prevention and control,       防和控制的主責單位，負責日常風險管理及內部
responsible for the implementation of daily risk management and internal            監控的實施、內外部風險資訊的識別與收集並協
control, identification and collection of internal and external risk information    助內審職能進行定期風險評估。高級管理層負責
and assisting the internal audit in performing regular risk assessments. The        制定適當原則及價值、進行風險評估及設計、執
senior management is responsible for setting appropriate principles and             行及維護內部監控，並向董事會提供風險管理及
values, performing risk assessments, owning the design, implementation and          內部監控有效性的確認。董事會及審核委員會負
maintenance of internal control, as well as giving confirmation to the Board on     責監督管理層的行動及監察監控系統的整體有效
the effectiveness of the risk management and internal control. The Board and        性。
the Audit Committee oversee the actions of the management and monitor the
overall effectiveness of the control systems.

Particular features of our risk management system include the following:            我們的風險管理系統包括下列特定的特點：

•     The Group has established a comprehensive three-level risk                    •   本集團已構建起一套完善的三級風險管理
      management system to accurately identify and assess major risks                   系 統，精 準 識 別 並 評 估 與 企 業 戰 略 目 標 相
      associated with our corporate strategies, goals and objectives. In the face       關 的 重 大 風 險。面 對 宏 觀 及 經 濟 風 險，我
      of macroeconomic and economic risks, we closely track policy directions           們 從 投 資 布 局、運 營 策 略、產 品 創 新、服

Risk Management Approach                                                              風險管理方針
Our approach to risk management is to identify current risk exposures and             我們的風險管理方針是識別現時風險承擔並隨後
manage them so that they can be understood, controlled or mitigated. All              對其進行監管，藉以瞭解、控制或減低有關風險。
business functions are required to identify material risks that may impact their      所有業務職能均須識別可能影響其策略及業務目
strategy and business objectives. They also aim to identify, assess, evaluate and     標的重大風險，亦致力於識別、評估、檢討及減
mitigate operational risks. Many aspects of risks are considered, including but       低營運風險。各方面的風險均被納入考慮，包括
not limited to business continuity, financial impact, reputational risk, safety and   但不限於業務持續性、財務影響、聲譽風險、安
health, external regulations (including the Listing Rules) and environmental,         全及健康、外部規例（包括上市規則）及與環境、
social and governance related risks.                                                  社會及管治相關的風險。

risk management and internal control systems are reported to the Board and            管理該等風險及弱點的處理計劃得以呈報予董事
the Audit Committee, along with action plans to manage these risks and                會及審核委員會。
weaknesses.

Midea Holding Co., Ltd. (“Midea Holding”) is directly wholly-owned by the             美的控股有限公司（「美的控股」）由本公司控股股
controlling shareholders of the Company, Mr. He and Ms. Lu, which owns and            東何先生及盧女士直接全資擁有，並透過若干間
operates high-end hotels through certain indirectly wholly-owned subsidiaries,        接全資附屬公司（其中一間為佛山市順德區君蘭
of which one is Foshan Shunde Junlan Holding Development Company                      控股發展有限公司）擁有及經營高端酒店。
Limited.

Hotel investment and operations is not the main stream business of Midea              酒店投資及運營並非美的控股及本集團的主要業
Holding and the Group. In order to diversify our earnings, we also engage             務。為增加我們的收益來源，我們亦參與經濟型
in the investment and operations of economy and cultural-tourism theme                及文化旅遊主題酒店的投資及營運。該等經濟型
hotels. The target customers of these economy and theme hotels are designed           及主題酒店的目標客戶分別為商務旅客及度假勝
for business travellers and tourists in the resort, respectively, which can be        地遊客，這有別於美的控股所擁有及運營的豪華
distinguished from that of the luxury hotels owned and operated by Midea              酒店的目標客戶。此外，董事會獨立於美的控股
Holding. Moreover, the Board is independent from the board of directors               的董事會，且何劍鋒先生及趙軍先生無法個人控
of Midea Holding, and Mr. He Jianfeng and Mr. Zhao Jun cannot personally              制董事會。另外，彼等完全知悉及一直履行其對
control the Board. Further, they are fully aware of, and have been discharging,       本公司的受信責任，並將繼續以本公司及其股東
their fiduciary duty to the Company and have acted and will continue to act in        的整體最佳利益行事。
the best interest of the Company and its shareholders as a whole.

      of the Cayman Islands. The address of the Company’s registered office                限 公 司。本 公 司 註 冊 辦 事 處 地 址 為 Walkers

      Cayman KY1-9008, Cayman Islands.                                                     Town, Grand Cayman KY1-9008, Cayman Islands。

      (the “Stock Exchange”) on 11 October 2018 (the “Listing”).                           市」）。

      Holding (BVI) Limited (“Midea Development (BVI)”), and the ultimate                  有 限 公 司（「美 的 發 展 (BVI) 」），本 公 司 的 最

      Parties”).

                initial recognition of asset and whether there has been                     慮 違 約 的 可 能 性，亦 評 估 資 產
                a significant increase in credit risk on an ongoing basis                   於各報告期間有否信用風險的
                throughout each reporting period. To assess whether                         持 續 顯 著 增 加。評 估 有 否 信 用
                there is a significant increase in credit risk the Group                    風 險 顯 著 增 加 時，本 集 團 將 報
                compares the risk of a default occurring on the asset as at                 告日期資產發生違約的可能性
                the reporting date with the risk of default as at the date                  與初始確認時發生違約的可能
                of initial recognition. It considers available reasonable and               性 進 行 比 較，同 時 考 慮 公 開 且
                supportive forwarding-looking information. Especially the                   合 理 的 前 瞻 性 資 料。以 下 指 標
                following indicators are incorporated:                                      需要重點考慮：

                •     actual or expected significant deterioration in external              •   金融資產外部信用評級的
                      credit rating of the financial assets                                     實際或預期出現重大惡化

                •     actual or expected significant adverse changes in                     •   營 業 狀 況、財 務 狀 況 和 經
                      business, financial or economic conditions that                           濟環境中的實際或預期重
                      are expected to cause a significant change to the                         大不利變化預期導致債務
                      debtor’s ability to meet its obligations                                  人償還到期債務的能力產
                                                                                                生重大變化

                •     actual or expected significant changes in the                         •   債務人經營業績實際或預
                      operating results of the debtor                                           期發生重大變化

                •     significant increases in credit risk on other financial               •   同一債務人的其他金融工
                      instruments of the same debtor                                            具信用風險顯著增加

                •     significant changes in the expected performance                       •   債務人預期表現或行為發
                      and behavior of the debtor, including changes in the                      生 重 大 變 化，包 括 債 務 人
                      payment status of debtor                                                  付款情況的變化。

         3.1.4 Liquidity risk                                                               3.1.4 流動資金風險
               Management of the Group aims at maintaining sufficient                             本集團的管理層旨在維持充足
               cash and cash equivalents or have available funding                                的現金及現金等價物或通過預
               through proceeds from pre-sale of properties and an                                售物業所得款項及充足的可用
               adequate amount of available financing including short-                            融資（包括短期及長期借款以及
               term and long-term borrowings and obtaining additional                             來自股東的額外融資）擁有可用
               funding from shareholders and monitors rolling forecasts of                        資 金，及 根 據 預 期 現 金 流 量 監
               the Group’s cash on the basis of expected cash flow. Due to                        察 本 集 團 現 金 之 滾 存 預 測。由
               the dynamic nature of the underlying businesses, the Group                         於 相 關 業 務 的 動 態 特 性，本 集
               maintains flexibility in funding by maintaining adequate                           團通過保持充足的現金及現金
               amount of cash and cash equivalents and through having                             等價物及擁有可用的融資來源

      3.2 Capital management                                                              3.2 資本管理
          The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard                           本集團管理資本旨在保障本集團持續
          the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order to                          經營的能力，藉以回報擁有人及使其
          provide returns for the owner and benefits for other stakeholders                       他利益相關者受益，同時維持最佳資
          and to maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce the cost of                      本構架以減少資本成本。
          capital.

          In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group                         為維持或調整資本結構，本集團可調
          may adjust the amount of dividends paid to the owner, issue new                         整派付予擁有人的股息款額、發行新
          shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                                                   股份或出售資產以降低債務。

          The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing ratio. This                      本集團根據淨負債率監控資本。該比
          ratio is calculated as net borrowings divided by total equity. Net                      率按借款淨額除以權益總額計算。借
          borrowings are calculated as total borrowings (including corporate                      款淨額按借款總額（包括合併資產負
          bonds and current and non-current bank and other borrowings                             債表所示的公司債券和即期及非即期
          as shown in the consolidated balance sheets) less total of cash                         銀行及其他借款）減現金及現金等價
          and cash equivalents, restricted cash and term deposits with initial                    物、受限制現金及原定到期日為三個
          terms of over three months.                                                             月以上的定期存款總額計算。

4.    Critical accounting estimates and judgements                                  4.   重要會計估計及判斷
      (a)   Estimates for fair value of investment properties                            (a)   投資物業公平值估計
            The Group assesses the fair value of its investment properties                     本集團根據獨立專業合資格估值師仲
            based on valuations determined by independent and professional                     量聯行釐定的估值評估其投資物業的
            qualified valuer Jones Lang LaSalle. Significant judgement                         公平值。評估投資物業的公平值須使
            and assumptions are required in assessing the fair value of the                    用重大判斷及假設，有關判斷及假設
            investment properties. Details of the judgements and assumptions                   詳情披露於附註 16。
            are disclosed in note 16.

      (b) Income taxes and deferred taxation                                             (b) 所得稅及遞延稅項
            The Group is subject to income taxes in the PRC. Significant                       本集團須繳納中國所得稅。釐定所得
            judgements are required in determining the provision for income                    稅撥備須作出重要判斷。在一般業務
            taxes. There are many transactions and calculations for which the                  過程中，許多交易及計算的最終釐定
            ultimate determination is uncertain during the ordinary course of                  尚不明朗。若最終稅款結果與最初記
            business. Where the final tax outcome of these matters is different                賬金額不同，有關差額將影響釐定年
            from the amounts that were initially recorded, such difference will                度的所得稅和遞延稅項撥備。
            impact the income tax and deferred tax provision in the year in
            which such determination is made.

            Deferred income tax assets relating to certain temporary                           當管理層認為將來可能有應課稅利潤
            differences and tax losses are recognised when management                          可抵銷暫時差額或可使用稅項虧損時，
            considers to be probable that future taxable profit will be available              確認有關若干暫時差額及稅項虧損的
            against which the temporary differences or tax losses can be                       遞延所得稅資產。實際應用結果可能
            utilised. The outcome of their actual utilisation may be different.                不同。

      (c)   Impairment of financial assets                                               (c)   金融資產減值
            The loss allowances for financial assets are based on assumptions                  金融資產虧損撥備乃基於有關違約風
            about risk of default and expected loss rates. The Group uses                      險及預期虧損率之假設作出。於各報
            judgements in making these assumptions and selecting the inputs                    告期末，本集團根據其過往歷史、現
            to the impairment calculation, based on the Group’s past history,                  時市況及前瞻性估計，通過判斷作出
            existing market conditions as well as forward looking estimates                    該等假設及選擇減值計算之輸入數據。
            at the end of each reporting period. Where the expectation is                      倘若預期與管理層的估計有差異，該
            different from the management’s estimate, such difference will                     差異將影響金融資產的賬面值及於估
            impact the carrying amount of the financial assets and the related                 計改變期間的相關虧損撥備。主要假

            Details of the key assumptions and inputs used are disclosed in                    3.1 的表格中披露。
            the tables in note 3.1.

      (b) Assets and liabilities related to contracts with                             (b) 與客戶合約有關的資產和負債
          customers
            Upon entering into a contract with a customer, the Group obtains                 在與客戶簽訂合約時，本集團獲得從
            rights to receive consideration from the customer and assumes                    客戶處收取代價的權利，並承擔向客
            performance obligations to transfer goods or provide services to                 戶轉讓貨物或提供服務的履約義務。
            the customer. The combination of those rights and performance                    根據剩餘權利和履約義務之間的關係，
            obligations gives rise to a net asset or a net liability depending               這些權利和履約義務的組合會產生淨
            on the relationship between the remaining rights and the                         資產或淨負債。如果剩餘權利的計量
            performance obligations. The contract is an asset and recognised                 超過剩餘履約義務的計量，則合約為
            as contract assets if the measure of the remaining rights                        資產並確認為合約資產。反之，如果
            exceeds the measure of the remaining performance obligations.                    剩餘履約義務的計量超過剩餘權利的
            Conversely, the contract is a liability and recognised as contract               計量，則合約為負債，並確認為合約
            liabilities if the measure of the remaining performance obligations              負債。
            exceeds the measure of the remaining rights.

16. Investment properties (Continued)                                              16. 投資物業（續）
      Valuation processes of the Group                                               本集團的估值程序
      A valuation of the Group’s investment properties as at 31 December             本集團於 2024 年及 2023 年 12 月 31 日的投資
      2024 and 2023 was carried out by the management or an independent              物業由管理層及持有相關認可專業資格的
      and professionally qualified valuer, Jones Lang LaSalle, who holds a           獨立及專業合資格估值師仲量聯行進行估
      recognised relevant professional qualification and has recent experience       值，有 關 估 值 師 近 期 對 所 估 值 投 資 物 業 的
      in the locations and nature of the investment properties being valued.         位置及性質有經驗。就所有投資物業而言，
      For all investment properties, their current use equates the highest and       彼等現有用途即最高及最佳用途。
      best use.

      Discussions of valuation processes and results were held between               管理層及估值師定期討論本集團中期及年
      management and the valuer on a regular basis, in line with the Group’s         度報告日期的估值程序及結果。
      the interim and annual reporting dates.

      At each financial year end, management:                                        於各財政年度末，管理層將：

      •     Verifies all major inputs to the independent valuation report;           •   核實獨立估值報告的所有重大輸入數
                                                                                         據；

      •     Assesses property valuation movements when compared to the               •   評估物業估值與上年度估值報告相比
            prior year valuation report; and                                             的變動；及

      •     Holds discussions with the independent valuer.                           •   與獨立估值師討論。

      Valuation techniques                                                           估值方法
      Valuations are based on:                                                       估值乃按以下方法進行：

      Income approach taking into account the current rents of the property          使 用 收 入 法，計 及 物 業 權 益 的 現 時 租 金 及
      interests and the reversionary potentials of the tenancies, term yield and     重 訂 租 約 的 可 能，隨 後 分 別 以 租 期 收 益 率
      reversionary yield are then applied respectively to derive the market          及復歸收益率計算物業的市值。
      value of the property.

      There were no changes to the valuation techniques during the year.             年內的估值方法並無變動。

    Relationships of unobservable inputs to fair value are as follows:                  不可觀察輸入數據與公平值的關係如下：

    •     The higher rate of return/capitalisation rate, the lower fair value;          •     回報率╱資本化比率越高，公平值越
                                                                                              低；

    •     The higher monthly rental, the higher fair value.                             •     月租越高，公平值越高。

33. Distribution in Specie                                                         33. 實物分派
      On 16 August 2024, the Company announced a proposed distribution               於 2024 年 8 月 16 日，本 公 司 宣 佈 建 議 以 實
      in specie of the entire interests of the direct wholly-owned subsidiary,       物方式分派直接全資附屬公司私人公司的
      the PrivateCo which are principally engaged in property development            全 部 權 益 予 本 公 司 股 東，私 人 公 司 主 要 透
      and sales business in PRC through its subsidiaries, to the shareholders        過其附屬公司於中國從事物業發展及銷售
      of the Company based on their shareholding percentage in the                   業 務，該 分 派 乃 按 彼 等 於 本 公 司 持 股 百 分
      Company. PrivateCo’s portfolio primarily consists of residential property      比 計 算。私 人 公 司 之 投 資 組 合 主 要 包 括 住
      development projects. To comply with the Listing Rules, the Company            宅 物 業 發 展 項 目。為 遵 守 上 市 規 則，本 公
      provided a cash consideration of HKD5.90 per share to the shareholders         司向選擇不收取私人公司股份之本公司股
      of the Company opting not to receive the shares of the PrivateCo (the          東 提 供 每 股 5.90 港 元 之 現 金 代 價（「現 金 替
      “Cash Alternative”) (collectively, the “Proposed Distribution”).               代」）（統稱「建議分派」）。

      In order to facilitate the Proposed Distribution, the Ultimate Controlling     為 促 成 建 議 分 派，最 終 控 股 方 及 美 的 發 展
      Parties and Midea Development (BVI) had undertaken to elect, accept            (BVI) 已 承 諾 選 擇、接 納 並 全 額 持 有 實 物 分
      and hold the shares of the PrivateCo under the Distribution in Specie in       派 項 下 的 私 人 公 司 股 份，並 透 過 向 本 公 司
      full and acquire shares of the PrivateCo not taken on by the shareholders      支 付 相 等 於 本 公 司 應 付╱已 付 現 金 替 代 金
      of the Company opting to receive cash under the Cash Alternative by            額之代價以收購選擇收取現金替代之本公
      paying to the Company a consideration equivalent to the amount of the          司股東所未承購之私人公司股份（「可能出
      Cash Alternative payable/paid by the Company (the “Possible Disposal”).        售事項」）。

      On 2 September 2024, the Proposed Distribution was approved by the             於 2024 年 9 月 2 日，股東特別大會批准建議
      extraordinary general meeting.                                                 分派。

      On 22 October 2024, the Company distributed the shares of the                  於 2024 年 10 月 22 日，本 公 司 將 私 人 公 司
      PrivateCo to the Ultimate Controlling Parties and the shareholders of          股份分派予最終控股方及於建議分派及可
      the Company who elect to obtain the shares of the PrivateCo upon               能出售事項完成後選擇取得私人公司股份
      the completion of the Proposed Distribution and the Possible Disposal.         之 本 公 司 股 東。因 此，根 據 香 港 財 務 報 告
      Accordingly, the profit from the PrivateCo for the period from 1 January       準 則 第 5 號「持 作 出 售 非 流 動 資 產 及 非 持
      2024 to 22 October 2024 was presented as Discontinued Operations               續 經 營 業 務」，私 人 公 司 於 2024 年 1 月 1 日
      in the consolidated statement of comprehensive income and the                  至 2024 年 10 月 22 日期間之利潤於合併綜合
      comparative figures of year ended 31 December 2023 were restated,              收 益 表 內 呈 列 為 非 持 續 經 營 業 務，而 截 至
      in accordance with HKFRS 5 “Non-current Assets Held for Sale and               2023 年 12 月 31 日止年度之比較數字則已重

43. Summary of other accounting policies                                            43. 其他會計政策概要
      43.1 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                          43.1 合併原則及權益會計處理
          (a)   Subsidiaries                                                            (a)   附屬公司
                Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured entities)                 附屬公司為本集團擁有控制權
                over which the Group has control. The Group controls                          的所有實體（包括結構性實體）。
                an entity where the Group is exposed to, or has rights to,                    本集團須承擔參與實體營運所
                variable returns from its involvement with the entity and has                 得 之 可 變 回 報 的 風 險，或 有 權
                the ability to affect those returns through its power to direct               獲 得 可 變 回 報，且 能 透 過 指 示
                the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are fully consolidated             實體業務的權力影響該等回報
                from the date on which control is transferred to the Group.                   時 對 實 體 擁 有 控 制 權。附 屬 公
                They are deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                    司於控制權轉移至本集團當日
                                                                                              起 全 面 合 併 入 賬，於 失 去 控 制
                                                                                              權當日終止合併入賬。

                The acquisition method of accounting is used to account                       本集團採用會計處理的收購法
                for business combinations by the Group (refer to note 43.2).                  將 業 務 合 併 入 賬（請 參 閱 附 註
                                                                                              43.2）。

                Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains                     集 團 內 公 司 間 交 易、集 團 公 司
                on transactions between group companies are eliminated.                       間交易的結餘及未變現收益會
                Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless the transaction                  予 以 對 銷。未 變 現 虧 損 亦 予 以
                provides evidence of an impairment of the transferred asset.                  對 銷，除 非 交 易 提 供 轉 讓 資 產
                Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been changed                         的 減 值 憑 證。附 屬 公 司 的 會 計
                where necessary to ensure consistency with the policies                       政 策 已 按 需 要 作 出 改 變，以 確
                adopted by the Group.                                                         保符合本集團採用的政策。

                Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of                        於附屬公司業績及權益的非控
                subsidiaries are shown separately in the consolidated                         制性權益分別列示於合併綜合
                statement of comprehensive income, statement of changes                       收 益 表、權 益 變 動 表 及 資 產 負
                in equity and balance sheet respectively.                                     債表。

          (b)   Associates                                                              (b)   聯營公司
                Associates are all entities over which the Group has                          聯營公司指本集團對其有重大
                significant influence but not control or joint control. This                  影響但無控制權或共同控制權
                is generally the case where the Group holds between 20%                       的所有實體，一般附帶擁有 20%
                and 50% of the voting rights. Investments in associates are                   至 50% 投票權。於聯營公司的投
                accounted for using the equity method of accounting (note                     資按成本初步確認後以權益會
                43.1(d)), after initially being recognised at cost.                           計法入賬（參見下文 (43.1(d))）。

        (c)   Joint arrangements                                                   (c)   合營安排
              Under HKFRS 11 Joint Arrangements investments in joint                     根 據 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 11 號
              arrangements are classified as either joint operations or                  合 營 安 排，投 資 視 乎 各 投 資 者
              joint ventures. The classification depends on the contractual              的合約權利和職責而非合營安
              rights and obligations of each investor, rather than the legal             排的法律形式，分為共同經營或
              structure of the joint arrangement. The Group has joint                    合營企業。本集團僅有合營企業。
              ventures only.

              method (note 43.1(d)), after initially being recognised at                 確認後於合併資產負債表以權
              cost in the consolidated balance sheet.                                    益法入賬（附註 43.1(d)）。

        (d)   Equity method                                                        (d)   權益法
              Under the equity method of accounting, the investments                     根 據 權 益 會 計 法，投 資 初 步 按
              are initially recognised at cost and adjusted thereafter to                成 本 確 認，其 後 經 調 整 以 確 認
              recognise the Group’s share of the post-acquisition profits                本集團應佔被投資公司收購後
              or losses of the investee in profit or loss, and the Group’s               損益及本集團應佔被投資公司
              share of movements in other comprehensive income of                        其 他 綜 合 收 益 變 動。已 收 或 應
              the investee in other comprehensive income. Dividends                      收聯營公司及合營企業股息確
              received or receivable from associates and joint ventures                  認為投資賬面值扣減。
              are recognised as a reduction in the carrying amount of the
              investment.

              Where the Group’s share of losses in an equity-accounted                   如本集團應佔權益入賬投資虧
              investment equals or exceeds its interest in the entity,                   損等於或超過應佔實體權益（包
              including any other unsecured long-term receivables,                       括任何其他無抵押長期應收款
              the Group does not recognise further losses, unless it has                 項），則 本 集 團 不 會 再 確 認 虧
              incurred obligations or made payments on behalf of the                     損，除 非 本 集 團 代 另 一 實 體 承
              other entity.                                                              擔責任或付款。

              Unrealised gains on transactions between the Group and its                 本集團與聯營公司及合營企業
              associates and joint ventures are eliminated to the extent of              交易的未變現收益按本集團所
              the Group’s interest in these entities. Unrealised losses are              佔該等實體權益進行對銷。除非
              also eliminated unless the transaction provides evidence of                交易提供轉讓資產的減值憑證，
              an impairment of the asset transferred. Accounting policies                否 則 未 變 現 虧 損 亦 予 以 對 銷。
              of equity-accounted investees have been changed where                      權益入賬的被投資公司的會計
              necessary to ensure consistency with the policies adopted                  政 策 已 按 需 要 作 出 改 變，以 確
              by the group.                                                              保符合本集團採用的政策。

              described in note 43.9.

          (e)   Changes in ownership interests                                         (e)   擁有權權益變動
                The Group treats transactions with non-controlling interests                 本集團將不會引致失去控制權
                that do not result in a loss of control as transactions with                 的非控制性權益交易視為與本

                results in an adjustment between the carrying amounts of                     權權益變動導致控制性權益與
                the controlling and non-controlling interests to reflect their               非控制性權益賬面值之間之調
                relative interests in the subsidiary. Any difference between                 整以反映彼等於附屬公司之相

                and any consideration paid or received is recognised in a                    與任何已付或已收對價間之任

                Group.                                                                       佔權益中之單獨儲備項目內確認。

                When the Group ceases to consolidate or equity account                       倘 本 集 團 因 喪 失 控 制 權、共 同
                for an investment because of a loss of control, joint control                控制權或重大影響力而終止就
                or significant influence, any retained interest in the entity                投 資 合 併 入 賬 或 按 權 益 入 賬，
                is remeasured to its fair value with the change in carrying                  其於該實體之任何保留權益按
                amount recognised in profit or loss. This fair value becomes                 其 公 平 值 重 新 計 量，而 賬 面 值
                the initial carrying amount for the purposes of subsequently                 變 動 則 於 損 益 內 確 認。就 隨 後
                accounting for the retained interest as an associate,                        入 賬 列 作 於 聯 營 公 司、合 營 企
                joint venture or financial asset. In addition, any amounts                   業或金融資產之保留權益而言，
                previously recognised in other comprehensive income                          公平值成為初步賬面值。此外，
                in respect of that entity are accounted for as if the Group                  先前於其他綜合收益確認該實
                had directly disposed of the related assets or liabilities. This             體的金額按猶如本集團已直接
                may mean that amounts previously recognised in other                         出售有關資產或負債的方式入
                comprehensive income are reclassified to profit or loss                      賬，可能意味著先前在其他綜合
                or transferred to another category of equity as specified/                   收益確認的金額按適用之香港
                permitted by applicable HKFRSs.                                              財務報告準則具體規定╱准許，

        (f)   Distribution in Specie                                                (f)   實物分派
              Upon loss of control of a subsidiary through distribution of                當本集團透過向本公司股東分
              shares in the subsidiary to the shareholders of the Company                 派附屬公司股份而失去對附屬
              where the subsidiary is ultimately controlled by the same                   公司的控制權時（如附屬公司在
              parties both before and after the distribution, the Group                   分派前後均由相同人士最終控
              (a) derecognises the assets and liabilities of the subsidiary               制），本集團 (a) 按失去控制權當
              at their carrying amounts at the date when control is lost;                 日的賬面值終止確認附屬公司
              and (b) measures the distribution against the equity to                     的 資 產 及 負 債；及 (b) 按 將 予 分
              its owners at the carrying amount of the net assets to be                   派淨資產的賬面值計量分派予

    43.6 Property, plant and equipment                                           43.6 物業、廠房及設備
        All property, plant and equipment are stated at historical cost less       所有物業、廠房及設備均以歷史成本

        attributable to the acquisition of the items.                              目直接相關之支出。

        or recognised as a separate asset, as appropriate, only when it is         經濟利益有可能流入本集團且該項目
        probable that future economic benefits associated with the item            的成本能可靠計量時，方計入資產之
        will flow to the Group and the cost of the item can be measured            賬面值或確認為獨立資產（如適用）。
        reliably. The carrying amount of the replaced part is derecognised.        取代部分的賬面值終止確認。所有其
        All other repairs and maintenance are charged to profit or loss            他維修及保養費用在產生的財政期間
        during the financial period in which they are incurred.                    於損益支銷。

        The leasehold improvements are depreciated over the shorter of              租賃物業裝修按其可使用年期或租期
        their useful life or the lease term, unless the Group expects to use       （以較短者為準）折舊，惟本集團預期
        the assets beyond the lease term.                                           在租期以外使用資產則另論。

        The assets’ residual values and useful lives are reviewed, and             本集團於各報告期末審閱資產的剩餘
        adjusted if appropriate, at the end of each reporting period.              價值和可使用年期，並作出適當調整。

        An asset’s carrying amount is written down immediately to its              倘資產賬面值高於估計可收回金額，
        recoverable amount if the asset’s carrying amount is greater than          則資產賬面值即時撇減至可收回金額。
        its estimated recoverable amount.

        loss.                                                                      損益。

              amount of goodwill relating to the entity sold.                             實體的損益包括有關出售實體
                                                                                          的商譽賬面值。

              Goodwill is allocated to cash-generating units for the                      商譽會被分配至現金產生單位
              purpose of impairment testing. The allocation is made to                    以 進 行 減 值 測 試。獲 分 配 商 譽
              those cash-generating units or groups of cash-generating                    的該等現金產生單位或現金產

      43.9 Impairment of non-financial assets                                      43.9 非金融資產減值
          Goodwill and intangible assets that have an indefinite useful              無確定可使用年期的商譽及無形資產
          life are not subject to amortisation and are tested annually               毋須攤銷，惟須每年進行減值測試，
          for impairment, or more frequently if events or changes in                 或倘有事件或情況變化顯示可能減值，
          circumstances indicate that they might be impaired. Other                  則會更頻密地進行測試。其他資產於
          assets are tested for impairment whenever events or changes                發生事件或情況有變顯示賬面值可能
          in circumstances indicate that the carrying amount may not be              無法收回時進行減值測試。減值虧損
          recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised for the amount               按資產賬面值超出可收回金額的金額
          by which the asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable               確認。可收回金額指資產公平值減出
          amount. The recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s fair            售成本及使用價值的較高者。就評估
          value less costs of disposal and value in use. For the purposes of         減值而言，資產按獨立可識別現金流
          assessing impairment, assets are grouped at the lowest levels              入的最低層次組合，該現金流入與其
          for which there are separately identifiable cash inflows which             他資產或資產組合（現金產生單位）的
          are largely independent of the cash inflows from other assets              現金流入很大程度上獨立開來。於各
          or groups of assets (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets          報告期末審閱已減值的非金融資產（商
          other than goodwill that suffered an impairment are reviewed for           譽除外）是否可能撥回減值。
          possible reversal of the impairment at the end of each reporting
          period.

      43.12 Offsetting financial instruments                                            43.12 抵銷金融工具
           Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount                  當有法定可執行權利可抵銷已確認金
           reported in the consolidated balance sheet when there is a legally              額，並有意圖按淨額基準結清或同時
           enforceable right to offset the recognised amounts and there is                 變現資產及結清負債時，有關金融資
           an intention to settle on a net basis or realise the asset and settle           產與負債可互相抵銷，並於合併資產
           the liability simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must not            負債表列報淨額。法定可執行權利不
           be contingent on future events and must be enforceable in the                   依賴未來事件而定，可於一般業務過

           or bankruptcy of the Company or the counterparty.                               無力償債或破產時強制執行。

    43.13 Impairment of financial assets                                               43.13 金融資產減值
         The Group assesses on a forward-looking basis the expected credit                 本集團按前瞻性基準評估與按攤銷成
         losses associated with its assets carried at amortised cost and debt              本計量的資產及按公平值計入其他綜
         investment at fair value through other comprehensive income.                      合收益的債務投資有關的預期信貸虧
         The impairment methodology applied depends on whether there                       損。應用的減值方法視乎信用風險有
         has been a significant increase in credit risk. Note 3 details how the            否 大 幅 增 加 而 定。附 註 3 詳 述 本 集 團
         Group determines whether there has been a significant increase                    如何確定信用風險有否大幅增加。預
         in credit risk. Expected credit losses are a probability-weighted                 期信貸虧損為於金融資產預期存續期
         estimate of credit losses (i.e. the present value of all cash shortfalls)         內對信貸虧損的概率 加權估計（即所
         over the expected life of the financial assets.                                   有現金短缺額的現值）。

         For trade receivables and contract assets, the Group applies                      對於貿易應收款項及合約資產，本集
         the simplified approach permitted by HKFRS 9, which requires                      團採用香港財務報告準則第 9 號允許
         expected lifetime losses to be recognised from initial recognition                的簡化方法，該方法要求初步確認資
         of the assets. The provision matrix is determined based on                        產時確認預期存續期虧損。撥備矩陣
         historical observed default rates over the expected life of the                   根據信用風險特徵相若的合約資產及
         contract assets and trade receivables with similar credit risk                    貿易應收款項預期存續期內的歷史觀
         characteristics and is adjusted for forward-looking estimates.                    察違約率釐定，並就前瞻性估計作出
         At every reporting date the historical observed default rates                     調整。本集團於各報告日期更新歷史
         are updated and changes in the forward-looking estimates are                      觀察違約率，並分析前瞻性估計的變
         analysed.                                                                         動。

         Impairment on other receivables from third parties, related                       來自第三方、關聯方及非控制性權益
         parties and non-controlling interests and financial guarantee are                 以及財務擔保的其他應收款項的減值
         measured as either 12-month expected credit losses or lifetime                    按 12 個月預期信貸虧損或整個存續期
         expected credit losses, depending on whether there has been                       的預期信貸虧損計量，視乎信用風險
         a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition. If a             自初步確認後有否顯著增加。倘應收

         since initial recognition, then impairment is measured as lifetime                加，則減值按整個存續期的預期信貸

    43.16 Contract assets, contract acquisition costs and                         43.16 合約資產、取得合約的成本與合
          contract liabilities                                                          約負債
         Upon entering into a contract with a customer, the Group obtains           與客戶簽訂合約時，本集團有權向客
         rights to receive consideration from the customer and assumes              戶收取對價，並承擔向客戶轉移貨物
         performance obligations to transfer goods or services to the               或服務的履約責任。該等權利與履約
         customer. The combination of those rights and performance                  責任的結合會產生淨資產或淨負債，
         obligations gives rise to a net asset or a net liability depending         取決於剩餘權利與履約責任的關係。
         on the relationship between the remaining rights and the                   倘收取對價的剩餘有條件權利的計量
         performance obligations. The contract is an asset and recognised           超過所履行的履約責任，則合約為資
         as contract assets if the measure of the remaining conditional             產並確認為合約資產。反之，倘餘下
         rights to consideration exceeds the satisfied performance                  履約責任的計量超過餘下權利，則合
         obligations. Conversely, the contract is a liability and recognised        約為負債並確認為合約負債。
         as contract liabilities if the measure of the remaining performance
         obligations exceeds the measure of the remaining rights.

         The Group recognises the incremental costs of obtaining a                  本集團在預期能收回自客戶取得合約
         contract with a customer as contract acquisition cost within               所增加成本的情況下於合約資產內確
         contract assets if the Group expects to recover these costs.               認有關成本為取得合約的成本。

      43.22 Borrowings                                                               43.22 借款
          Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of transaction          借款初步按公平值扣除交易成本確認，
          costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently carried at amortised               其 後 按 攤 銷 成 本 列 賬，所 得 款 項（扣
          cost; any difference between the proceeds (net of transaction                  除交易成本）與贖回價值的任何差額
          costs) and the redemption value is recognised in profit or loss over           使用實際利率法於借款期間在損益確
          the period of the borrowings using the effective interest method.              認。

          Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are recognised as            在貸款很有可能部分或全部提取的情
          transaction costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable that           況下，設立貸款融資所支付的費用確
          some or all of the facility will be drawn down. In this case, the fee          認為貸款交易成本，遞延至提取貸款
          is deferred until the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no              為止。如無法證明該貸款很有可能部
          evidence that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be          分或全部提取，則有關費用作為流動
          drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a pre-payment for liquidity              資金服務的預付款項撥充資本，並於
          services and amortised over the period of the facility to which it             有關融資期間攤銷。
          relates.

          Borrowings are derecognised when the obligation specified in                   當合約規定的責任終止、撤銷或屆滿
          the contract is extinguished, cancelled or expired. The difference             時，借款即終止確認。已償清或轉移
          between the carrying amount of a financial liability that has been             至另一方的金融負債賬面值與已付對
          extinguished or transferred to another party and the consideration             價（包括已轉讓非現金資產或所承擔
          paid, including any non-cash assets transferred or liabilities                 負債）之間的差額於損益確認為融資
          assumed, is recognised in profit or loss as finance costs.                     成本。

          Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless, at the end of         除非在報告期末本集團有權將負債延
          the reporting period, the group has a right to defer settlement of             遲至報告期後至少 12 個月償還，否則
          the liability for at least 12 months after the reporting period.               借款分類為流動負債。

          Covenants that the group is required to comply with, on or before              在將附帶契諾的貸款安排分類為流動
          the end of the reporting period, are considered in classifying                 或非流動時，需要考慮本集團須於報
          loan arrangements with covenants as current or non-current.                    告期末或之前須遵守的契諾。本集團
          Covenants that the group is required to comply with after the                  須於報告期後須遵守的契諾並不影響
          reporting period do not affect the classification at the reporting             於報告日期的分類。
          date.

              and considers whether it is probable that a taxation                           可能稅務機關將接納不確定的

      43.27 Provisions                                                                 43.27 撥備
           Provisions for legal claims, service warranties and make good                   當本集團因過往事件須承擔現有法律
           obligations are recognised when the group has a present legal or                或推定責任，而解除責任很可能導致
           constructive obligation as a result of past events, it is probable that         資源流出，且金額能可靠估算時，會
           an outflow of resources will be required to settle the obligation               確認法律申索、服務保證及妥善履責
           and the amount can be reliably estimated. Provisions are not                    撥備，但不會就未來經營虧損確認任
           recognised for future operating losses.                                         何撥備。

           Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood                 倘有多項同類責任，則會整體考慮責
           that an outflow will be required in settlement is determined by                 任類別以釐定履行責任時可能流出的
           considering the class of obligations as a whole. A provision is                 資源。即使同一責任類別所涉及任何
           recognised even if the likelihood of an outflow with respect to any             一個項目相關的資源流出可能極低，
           one item included in the same class of obligations may be small.                仍須確認撥備。

           Provisions are measured at the present value of management’s                    撥備按管理層於年末履行當前責任所
           best estimate of the expenditure required to settle the present                 需支出的最佳估計的現值計量。用於
           obligation at the end of the year. The discount rate used to                    釐定現值的貼現率為反映當前市場對
           determine the present value is a pre-tax rate that reflects current             貨幣時間價值及責任特定風險之評估
           market assessments of the time value of money and the risks                     的稅前利率。隨時間流逝而增加的撥
           specific to the liability. The increase in the provision due to the             備確認為利息開支。
           passage of time is recognised as interest expense.

    43.31 Leases                                                                  43.31 租賃
         (a)   The Group as the lessee                                                (a)    本集團作為承租人
               Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease                                合約可能包含租賃及非租賃組
               components. The Group allocates the consideration in the                      成 部 分。本 集 團 以 租 賃 及 非 租
               contract to the lease and non-lease components based on                       賃組成部分的相對獨立價格為
               their relative stand-alone prices. However, for leases of real                基 準，將 合 約 的 對 價 分 配 至 租
               estate for which the Group is a lessee, it has elected not                    賃及非租賃組成部分。然而，就
               to separate lease and non-lease components and instead                        本集團為承租人的不動產租賃
               accounts for these as a single lease component.                               而 言，其 選 擇 不 將 租 賃 及 非 租
                                                                                             賃 組 成 部 分 分 開，而 就 此 入 賬
                                                                                             為單獨租賃部分。

               Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and                         租 賃 條 款 按 個 別 基 準 磋 商，並
               contain a wide range of different terms and conditions. The                   包 含 多 項 不 同 的 條 款 及 條 件。
               lease agreements do not impose any covenants other than                       除出租人持有的租賃資產擔保
               the security interests in the leased assets that are held by                  權 益 外，租 賃 協 議 並 無 施 加 任
               the lessor. Leased assets may not be used as security for                     何 契 約。租 賃 資 產 不 可 作 為 借
               borrowing purposes.                                                           款用途的抵押品。

      43.31 Leases (Continued)                                                       43.31 租賃（續）
          (a)   The Group as the lessee (Continued)                                     (a)   本集團作為承租人（續）
                The lease payments are discounted using the interest                          租賃付款使用租賃所隱含的利
                rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily                    率貼現。倘無法釐定該利率，則
                determined, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is                        使 用 承 租 人 增 量 借 款 利 率，即
                used, being the rate that the individual lessee would have                    個別承租人為在類似經濟環境
                to pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset of                    中 以 類 似 條 款、抵 押 及 條 件 獲
                similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic                 得類似價值使用權資產所需借
                environment with similar terms, security and conditions.                      入的資金須支付的利率。

                To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group:                       為釐定增量貸款利率，本集團：

                •     where possible, uses recent third-party financing                       •   在 可 能 情 況 下，使 用 個 別
                      received by the individual lessee as a starting point,                      承租人最近獲得的第三方
                      adjusted to reflect changes in financing conditions                         融資為出發點作出調整以
                      since third party financing was received                                    反映自獲得第三方融資以
                                                                                                  來融資條件的變動

                •     uses a build-up approach that starts with a risk-free                   •    使 用 累 加 法，首 先 就 本 集
                      interest rate adjusted for credit risk for leases held by                    團所持有租賃的信用風險
                      the Group, which does not have recent third party                           （最近並無第三方融資）調
                      financing, and                                                              整無風險利率；及

                •     makes adjustments specific to the lease, e.g. term,                     •   進 行 特 定 於 租 約 的 調 整，
                      country, currency and security                                              例 如 期 限、國 家、貨 幣 及
                                                                                                  抵押

                If a readily observable amortising loan rate is available to the              若個別承租人通過最近的融資
                individual lessee (through recent financing or market data)                   或市場數據可獲得易觀察的攤
                which has a similar payment profile to the lease, then the                    銷 貸 款 利 率，且 該 利 率 與 租 賃
                group entities use that rate as a starting point to determine                 的 付 款 概 況 類 似，則 集 團 實 體
                the incremental borrowing rate.                                               以該利率作為起點釐定增量借
                                                                                              款利率。

                Right-of-use assets are generally depreciated over the                        使用權資產乃按資產可使用年
                shorter of the asset’s useful life and the lease term on                      期 及 租 期（以 較 短 者 為 準）按 直
                a straight-line basis. If the group is reasonably certain                     線 基 準 折 舊。倘 本 集 團 合 理 地
                to exercise a purchase option, the right-of-use asset is                      確定行使採購權利，使用權資產
                depreciated over the underlying asset’s useful life. While                    在相關資產可使用年期內折舊。
                the group revalues its land and buildings that are presented                  儘 管 本 集 團 重 估 其 在 物 業、廠
                within property, plant and equipment, it has chosen not to                    房及設備中呈列的土地及樓宇，
                do so for the right-of-use buildings held by the Group.                       但本集團選擇不對其持有的使
                                                                                              用權樓宇進行重估。

                Lease classification is made at the inception date and is                    租 賃 分 類 乃 於 開 始 日 期 作 出，
                reassessed only if there is a lease modification.                            且僅於存在租賃修改時方進行
                                                                                             重新評估。

                A finance lease is a lease that the Group as the lessor uses                 融資租賃指本集團（作為出租人）
                to transfer substantially all the risks and rewards incidental               將租賃資產所有權附帶的絕大
                to ownership of the leased asset to the lessee. At the                       部分風險及回報轉移至承租人
                commencement of the lease term, the Group recognises                         租賃。於租期開始時，本集團將
                the minimum lease amounts receivable by the Group as                         本集團應收的最低租賃款項確
                a finance lease receivable and records the unguaranteed                      認 為 融 資 租 賃 應 收 款 項，並 將
                residual value as an asset within the same category. The                     無擔保剩餘價值記錄為同一類
                difference between (a) the aggregate of the minimum                          別 資 產。(a) 最 低 租 賃 款 項 及 無
                lease amounts and the unguaranteed residual value and (b)                    擔 保 剩 餘 價 值 的 總 和 與 (b) 其 現
                their present value (presented in the consolidated balance                   值（於合併資產負債表中作為融
                sheet as finance lease receivables — net) is recognised as                   資租賃應收款項 — 淨額呈報）之
                unearned finance income. Minimum lease amounts are the                       間 的 差 額，確 認 為 未 賺 取 財 務
                payments over the lease term that the lessee is or can be                    收 益。最 低 租 賃 款 項 指 租 期 內
                required to make plus any residual value guaranteed to the                   承租人須或可能須支付的款項，
                lessor by the lessee, or a party unrelated to the lessor.                    加上承租人或與出租人無關的
                                                                                             人士向出租人擔保的任何剩餘
                                                                                             價值。

                Unearned finance income is allocated to each period                          未賺取財務收益乃使用實際利
                during the lease term using the effective interest method                    率法於租賃期內分配至各期間，
                that allocates each rental between finance income and                        並於各會計期間在財務收益與
                repayment of capital in each accounting period in such                       償 還 資 本 之 間 分 配 各 項 租 金，
                a way that finance income is recognised as a constant                        令財務收益確認為出租人於租
                periodic rate of return (implicit effective interest rate) on                賃淨投資的固定週期回報率（隱
                the lessor’s net investment in the lease. Initial direct costs,              含實際利率）。初始直接成本（如
                such as commissions, legal fees and internal costs that are                  磋商及安排租賃所增加及直接
                incremental and directly attributable to negotiating and                     應 佔 的 佣 金、法 律 費 用 及 內 部
                arranging a lease, are included in the initial measurement                   成本）已計入融資租賃應收款項
                of the finance lease receivable and reduce the amount of                     的 初 始 計 量，並 減 少 租 賃 期 內
                income recognised over the lease term.                                       所確認的收入金額。

                A lease is classified as an operating lease if it does not                   倘一項租賃不會將租賃資產所
                transfer substantially all the risks and rewards incidental                  有權附帶的絕大部分風險及回
                to ownership of the leased asset to the lessee. Amounts                      報 轉 移 至 承 租 人，則 該 項 租 賃
                received from lessees under operating leases (net of                         分類為經營租賃。根據經營租賃
                any incentives grant to the lessee) are recognised in the                    自承租人所得款項（扣除授予承
                consolidated statement of comprehensive income on a                          租人的任何優惠）於租期內按直
                straight-line basis over the period of the lease.                            線基準於合併綜合收益表內確認。

      43.33 Financial guarantee contracts                                               43.33 財務擔保合約
          Financial guarantee contracts are recognised as a financial                      財務擔保合約於發出擔保時確認為金
          liability at the time the guarantee is issued. The liability is initially        融負債。負債初步按公平值計量，其
          measured at fair value and subsequently at the higher of                         後按以下之較高者計量：

          •     the amount determined in accordance with the expected                      •   根據香港財務報告準則第 9 號「金
                credit loss model under HKFRS 9, ‘Financial Instruments’ and                   融工具」之預期信用虧損模型釐
                                                                                               定的金額及

          •     the amount initially recognised less, where appropriate, the               •   初 步 確 認 金 額 減（如 適 用）根 據
                cumulative amount of income recognised in accordance                           香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 15 號 與 客
                with the principles of HKFRS 15 Revenue from Contracts                         戶的合約收益所確認累計收入
                with Customers.                                                                金額。

          The fair value of financial guarantees on initial recognition equals             財務擔保於初始確認時的公平值等於
          the present value of the premium in an arm’s length transaction.                 公平交易中保證金的現值。

          Where guarantees in relation to loans or other payables of                       倘聯營公司的貸款或其他應付款項擔
          associates are provided for no compensation, the fair values are                 保為無償提供，相關公平值作為注資
          accounted for as contributions and recognised as part of the cost                入賬，確認為投資成本一部分。
          of the investment.
